### 2026-09-16 17:14:29  #公司 #國際

【亞馬遜計劃到2031年將當日達履約網點擴充至1000個以上】亞馬遜正籌劃一項前所未有的當日達配送業務擴張計劃，或將重塑美國零售格局，正面挑戰沃爾瑪在日常消費品領域最大的競爭優勢。 亞馬遜內部規劃檔案獲悉，這項代號為“水星計劃（Project Mercury）”的專案目標是到2031年，將亞馬遜當日達履約網點擴充至1000個以上，相比目前約85個站點，規模增長超十倍。 該專案耗資將達數十億美元，有望改變美國人採購生鮮食品、紙巾、止咳藥等日常用品的消費模式。 沃爾瑪憑藉遍佈美國消費者周邊的數千家大型門店，長期佔據這塊高粘性、高價值的市場。亞馬遜押注，透過新建數百個當日達履約中心（SSD），把庫存前置到離消費者更近的地方，改寫日用零售的競爭規則。這類履約中心儲備平臺9萬款最熱銷商品。

### 2026-09-16 17:12:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 2

Euro Area Industrial Production Falls Less than Expected — Euro Area industrial production declined 0.1% in July 2026, matching the fall recorded in June but performing slightly better than the 0.2% contraction expected by markets. The monthly decline reflected weaker output of non-durable consumer goods, which fell 1.6% (vs 1.8% in June) while energy production growth slowed to 0.9% from 1.4%. In contrast, production of intermediate goods recovered by 0.3% after a 0.9% decline, while capital-goods output increased 0.5%, reversing the previous month’s 1.8% drop. Durable consumer-goods production also strengthened, rising 0.9% from 0.4% previously. Performance varied across the bloc’s largest economies, with industrial output falling 1.5% in Germany and 0.4% in France, while Spain recorded a 0.6% increase. On an annual basis, Euro Area industrial production was unchanged in July, slightly outperforming expectations for a 0.1% decline.

【Japan August oil import costs surge 58.7%; terms of trade likely to worsen】Japan's August oil import bill rose 58.7% YoY while import volumes increased only 3.6% YoY, lifting overall imports 28% YoY and widening the trade deficit for a fourth consecutive month. ### 2026-09-16 17:06:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Hover Around 3-Week Low — Cocoa futures traded around $5,900 per tonne, holding close to their lowest since August 26, amid signs of abundant short-term supply. ICE cocoa inventories stand at a two-year high of around 3.42 million bags. Moreover, Ivory Coast cocoa shipments reached 2.14 million metric tons in the current international marketing year through Sept. 13, up 18% from a year earlier, according to Bloomberg. Meanwhile, the market continues to monitor crop diseases, excessive rainfall, and plantation conditions across West Africa. The outlook for the 2026/27 season remains uncertain, with weather risks, particularly the development of the El Niño weather pattern, threatening crops in the Ivory Coast and Ghana. This year’s strong El Niño could intensify the dry harmattan winds, which typically arrive in the region around November. This would mark a shift from excessive moisture to conditions that could threaten the final stages of the main crop, which typically runs from October to March.

【全球搶鎢潮 上游鎢礦企業賺翻了】上海鋼聯鎢行業分析師呂彥楠表示，鎢價上漲最核心的驅動因素是供給剛性約束疊加需求釋放造成的供需錯配，價格自礦端持續上行。 價格飆升背後，鎢的戰略地位也在飛躍。據東方證券8月20日研報，各國對戰略資源的囤積與保護將持續壓縮全球流通中的可得鎢資源總量，放大價格彈性。面對全球“搶鎢”熱潮，中國優勢依然穩固。中金公司7月29日釋出的研報顯示，中國國內APT、鎢粉加工費均值較2025年分別上漲331%、447%，出口結構向高附加值轉移，海外溢價創歷史新高。 據Wind統計，A股鎢礦核心標的為廈門鎢業、中鎢高新、章源鎢業、翔鷺鎢業。2026上半年四家企業盈利大幅增長，呂彥楠指出，上游採選企業是最大受益者，但受配額限制，增量空間有限。（中新經緯）

【上海國際能源交易中心擬修訂實際控制關係賬戶管理細則】上海國際能源交易中心就修訂《上海國際能源交易中心實際控制關係賬戶管理細則》公開徵求意見。具體修訂內容如下：一是新增實際控制關係認定情形。二是完善疑似實際控制關係賬戶詢問程式。三是完善否認實際控制關係後的處理程式。四是最佳化規則表述，與上位和配套規則保持一致。五是最佳化與《上海國際能源交易中心違規處理實施細則》之間的制度銜接。六是表述調整。包括明確“開戶機構”定義（期貨公司會員、境外特殊經紀參與者、境外中介機構統稱開戶機構）並統一相關表述，調整中國期貨市場監控中心有限責任公司的全稱和簡稱、保持“非期貨公司會員、境外特殊非經紀參與者、客戶”各條款順序一致，更新細則修訂記錄，以及刪除原附《合規宣告及承諾書》（改由上期能源官網釋出）等。

【丁薛祥出席平陸運河通航儀式】9月16日上午，中共中央政治局常委、國務院副總理丁薛祥在廣西欽州出席平陸運河通航儀式。通航儀式在平陸運河馬道樞紐舉行，廣西壯族自治區、交通運輸部主要負責同志和工程建設者代表現場發言。10時25分，丁薛祥宣佈平陸運河通航，輪船鳴笛啟航。丁薛祥在實地察看運河建設情況時表示，習近平總書記高度重視平陸運河建設，多次作出重要指示批示，要求高標準、高質量建設平陸運河，打造優質工程、綠色工程、廉潔工程。經過4年艱苦奮戰，平陸運河順利建成通航，承載著黨中央的殷切期望，得益於各有關方面和人民群眾的大力支援，凝聚了廣大建設者的辛勤汗水。要以運河建成通航為契機，高水平打造北部灣國際門戶港，推動西部地區擴大開放，為構建新發展格局作出更大貢獻。丁薛祥還登船巡航考察運河航道執行、沿岸生態環境保護等情況。他指出，平陸運河作為江鐵海多式聯運的重大基礎設施，是西部陸海新通道的骨幹工程。要做好運營管理，加強運力保障和貨源組織，充分發揮陸海統籌、內外聯動優勢，讓這條通江達海的黃金水道安全高效執行，實現經濟效益、社會效益、生態效益有機統一。平陸運河連線廣西南寧和北部灣，有力支援廣西向海圖強、開放發展，為中國和東盟加強互聯互通、高質量共建“一帶一路”注入新動能。（新華社）

【徐湖平等相關責任人被依規依紀依法處理】 記者從江蘇省有關部門獲悉，南京博物院相關受贈文物管理問題所涉責任單位和人員已被依規依紀依法處理。 徐湖平利用擔任南京博物院常務副院長、院長等職務上的便利，為他人在承接工程、促成商業合作等方面提供幫助，收受他人財物，犯受賄罪，被依法判處有期徒刑三年，並處罰金人民幣二十萬元。此前，根據南京博物院相關受贈文物管理問題調查情況，已依規依紀依法給予徐湖平開除黨籍處分，按規定取消其享受的待遇。原省文化廳、省文物局、南京博物院、原省文物總店張某等相關責任人，因履行崗位職責不力或執法監督責任不到位，對違規調撥銷售書畫等問題負有相應領導責任或直接責任，分別給予開除、撤職、降級、黨內嚴重警告、黨內警告、調整職務以及批評教育等相應處理。 省文旅廳、省文物局、南京博物院在向省委省政府作出深刻檢查的同時，認真汲取教訓，正在舉一反三在全省範圍內深入推進國有博物館館藏文物安全管理專項治理，切實改進完善文物管理制度，守牢文物安全底線。(新華社)

【日本連續4個月出現貿易逆差】日本財務省16日公佈的初步統計結果顯示，受原油等進口商品成本上漲影響，日本8月貿易逆差達1.11萬億日元（1美元約合155日元），已連續4個月出現貿易逆差。資料顯示，日本8月進口額為11.15萬億日元，同比上升28.0%。其中，由於進口量回升疊加進口單價居高不下，原油進口額同比大增58.7%，來自美國的原油進口額約為去年同期的11倍，達4004億日元。8月日本原油進口量為1159.4萬千升，同比增加3.6%。其中，來自中東地區的原油進口量同比下降30.9%，為725萬千升，連續5個月低於去年同期；來自美國的原油進口量達365萬千升，約為去年同期的7倍，連續5個月高於去年同期。此外，受半導體等電子零部件、汽車、半導體制造裝置等出口增長推動，8月日本出口額同比增長19.3%至10.05萬億日元。（新華社）

### 2026-09-16 16:55:08  #公司 #國際

【馮德萊恩：歐盟擬限制未成年人使用社交媒體】當地時間9月16日，歐盟委員會主席馮德萊恩在法國斯特拉斯堡歐洲議會發表年度“盟情諮文”時宣佈，歐盟將進一步加強對未成年人網路使用的保護，並提出分年齡段限制未成年人使用社交媒體等數字服務。馮德萊恩表示，13歲以下兒童不得使用社交媒體，15歲以下未成年人不得擁有個人社交媒體賬戶。對於13歲至15歲的未成年人，歐盟擬允許其使用由父母或監護人開設並監管的“迷你賬戶”，同時限制賬戶功能和使用時間。她還表示，對於15歲至18歲的未成年人，數字平臺將必須履行“安全設計”義務。馮德萊恩強調，歐盟“不應接受”容易讓人上癮的網路功能，也不應接受兒童不斷接觸更加極端的網路內容。她表示，未來將改變平臺與使用者之間的責任關係，由數字平臺證明其服務對兒童是安全的，而不是由兒童和家長承擔全部風險。據介紹，歐盟委員會將於9月17日公佈相關“兒童網路法案”（Kids Act）的具體方案。相關措施還需經過歐盟成員國和歐洲議會等程式後才能最終落地。（央視新聞）

【五大發電集團總裝機容量超15億千瓦，佔比37%】9月16日，國內五大發電集團之一的國家電投集團對外發布訊息，截至2026年8月底，公司發電總裝機容量突破3億千瓦，達到3.13億千瓦。 記者梳理公開資料瞭解到，國家電投當前裝機規模在國內五大發電集團中位居第二位。排在首位的國家能源集團，截至2026年6月，發電總裝機已超4億千瓦，達40000.6萬千瓦。 根據行業機構測算，預計2026年日本全國發電裝機規模為3.97億千瓦。對比可以看出，僅國家能源集團一家企業的總裝機規模，就已經超過日本全國的發電裝機總量。 記者查詢官方資料顯示，截至2025年底，五大發電集團其餘三家——中國華能、中國華電、中國大唐的總裝機容量分別為3億千瓦、2.81億千瓦、2.14億千瓦。 根據上述官方資料，截至目前，五大發電集團的總裝機容量已經超過15億千瓦，相當於全國的37%。（第一財經）

### 2026-09-16 16:46:54  #市場 #國際

【美欲“大手筆”供以重磅炸彈 被批助紂為虐 】美國媒體15日披露，美國政府正著手向以色列提供價值28億美元的彈藥，包括數萬枚具有高度破壞力的2000磅（約907公斤）炸彈。這一軍售計劃備受批評。《華盛頓郵報》等媒體援引匿名聯邦官員及知情人士的話報道，美方擬提供的彈藥包括4萬枚2000磅炸彈，其中MK-84型炸彈、BLU-117型炸彈各2萬枚，另有2萬枚I-2000型鑽地彈頭。美國政府已向國會非正式通報這項軍售計劃。這一軍售大部分資金將透過“外國軍事融資”專案支付，即美國以贈款或貸款形式向以色列提供資金，再由以方用這筆資金從美國購買武器。實質上是美國政府付錢採購炸彈給以色列使用。《華盛頓郵報》在報道中指出，2000磅炸彈是西方武器庫中破壞力最強的彈藥之一。一枚MK-84型炸彈的殺傷範圍達數百米，能破壞厚重的混凝土或金屬結構，在地面留下巨大彈坑。民主黨籍前總統拜登執政時，出於對巴勒斯坦平民傷亡的擔憂，曾暫停向以方提供這類重磅炸彈。報道指出，以軍在加沙地帶和黎巴嫩的衝突中數以百次計地使用2000磅炸彈。另有媒體稱，以軍甚至為了清除一個單人目標而不惜使用重磅炸彈，從而造成大量平民傷亡。（新華社）

### 2026-09-16 16:20:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Italian August Inflation Confirmed at 3-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Italy rose to 3.3% in August 2026 from 2.9% in July, confirming the preliminary estimate and marking the highest reading since September 2023. The main upward contribution came from higher energy prices, with regulated energy inflation rising to 18.6% from 14.8% and non-regulated energy inflation increasing to 17.0% from 11.4%, as ongoing tensions in the Middle East continued to fuel inflationary pressures. Meanwhile, inflation for durable goods accelerated to 1.3% from 0.7%, while unprocessed food inflation rose to 3.8% from 3.6%. In contrast, inflation eased for recreational, cultural and personal care services (2.6% vs 3%), and transport services (0.8% vs 1.6%). Core inflation, excluding energy and fresh food, edged down to 1.5% from 1.6%. Monthly, the CPI rose 0.5%, following a 0.3% gain in July. The HICP inflation accelerated to 3.2% year-on-year from 2.9% in July, confirming the preliminary estimate, while rising 0.1% month-on-month.

Meanwhile, production eased in the supply of electricity, gas, steam, hot water and air conditioning sector (13.3% vs 22.7%) and water supply; collection, treatment and disposal of waste, activities for the elimination of pollution sector (6.9% vs 7.8%). Output increased in sixteen regions of the republic, while declines were recorded in Akmola, Atyrau, Batys Kazakhstan and Shygys Kazakhstan regions. On a monthly basis, industrial production surged 14%, rebounding sharply from a 6.1% fall in July and reaching its highest reading since March.

Sweden Household Spending Stood at 0.7% MoM — Household spending in Sweden rose 0.7% month-on-month in August 2026, unchanged from the previous month. Spending accelerated on transportation (2.1% vs 0.1% in July), housing and utilities (0.4% vs 0.1%), and recreation, sports, and culture (0.5% vs 0.1%), while it rebounded on clothing and footwear (1.1% vs -0.6%), furnishings, household equipment, and routine home maintenance (5.4% vs -1.3%), healthcare (0.4% vs -0.7%), information and communication (1.2% vs -1.5%), restaurants and accommodation (1.7% vs -0.2%), and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (2.4% vs -2.3%). In contrast, spending declined on food and non-alcoholic beverages (-0.1% vs 0.4%) and alcoholic beverages, tobacco, and drugs (-3.4% vs 1.0%). On a yearly basis, household consumption climbed 4.0% in August 2026, remaining at its fastest pace since May 2022 and matching the growth rate recorded in the previous two months.

【SEBI eases arbitrage fund limits, allows temporary unhedged holdings up to 1%】SEBI has relaxed position limits for arbitrage mutual funds, permitting them to hold temporarily up to 1% of a scheme's assets unhedged. The change aims to inject liquidity into the closing auction, which has struggled to attract sufficient participation since its launch on Aug 3. Assets under management in arbitrage funds stood at 3 tln rupees (USD 31bn) at end-August. Previously, funds were required to remain fully hedged, limiting auction participation; under the prior framework price moves in the final 30 minutes created dislocations between spot and futures that arbitrageurs could close, and the shortened closing auction narrowed that intervention window, raising the risk of temporary mismatches.

Swedish Jobless Rate Rises to 8.5% — Sweden’s unemployment rate rose to 8.5% in August 2026 from 8.4% in the same period a year earlier. The total number of unemployed people increased by 19.3 thousand from August 2025 to 505.2 thousand, which consists of 251 thousand unemployed women and 255 thousand unemployed men, corresponding to 8.9% and 8.2%, respectively. Among people aged 15-24, 144 thousand were unemployed, corresponding to a youth unemployment rate of 18.4%. The number of long-term unemployed, defined as those jobless for at least 27 weeks, fell by 36 thousand to 168 thousand, with women accounting for 73,000 and men 95,000. Meanwhile, employment among people aged 15-74 rose by 102.6 thousand from a year earlier to 5.4 million, including 2.58 million women, up 61,000, and 2.85 million men. The average total number of hours worked per week stood at 128.7 million. On a seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate was 8.7%, while the youth unemployment stood at 23.8%.

【UK inflation rises for second month, adds pressure on BoE】UK consumer inflation rose for a second consecutive month in August as vehicle fuel jumped, adding pressure on the Bank of England ahead of its policy decision. The ONS said CPI rose 3.1% YoY in August, the highest since March and up from July’s 2.9%; the print matched economists’ median and exceeded the BoE’s prior 2.8% projection. The acceleration was driven by higher petrol and diesel prices linked to the ongoing Iran-related Middle East conflict; global oil is trading above $100/bbl and UK pump prices are at their most expensive since 2022. Sterling held gains after the release. BoE officials meeting this week are expected to keep rates unchanged, citing a weak labour market that has so far contained spillovers; Governor Bailey warned that a prolonged conflict, UK drought and an imminent El Nifio could raise food-price risks.

### 2026-09-16 14:10:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Extends Decline to 8-Month Low — Nickel traded around $16,000 per tonne, falling to its lowest level since December 2025 as Indonesia cut benchmark prices for low-grade nickel ore. The revised pricing formula nearly halves the cost of 1.2% nickel ore, improving the economics of high-pressure acid leach (HPAL) plants, while significant new capacity is expected to ramp up this year and next, potentially adding to global supply. Additionally, stronger US rate-hike bets lifted the dollar and Treasury yields, weighing on base metals broadly. Over the longer term, expected US deep-sea mining permits could eventually pave the way for additional nickel, cobalt, copper and manganese supply. Meanwhile, drought conditions in Indonesia have constrained Nickel Industries' Excelsior Nickel Cobalt facility to 30% of nameplate capacity, while output at the Indonesia Morowali Industrial Park could fall 30% to 40% if new water sources are not secured, limiting near-term supply growth.

### 2026-09-16 14:06:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

UK Inflation Accelerates to 3.1% — The UK’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.1% in August 2026, the highest level in five months, in line with market expectations and up from 2.9% in July. Transport costs, particularly motor fuels, made the largest upward contribution to the annual rate, with transport inflation rising to 4.6% from 3.6% in July. Petrol prices rose 9.1 pence per litre to 161.3 pence, while diesel prices increased 14.2 pence to 181.8 pence, pushing motor fuel inflation to 23.0% from 15.5%. Housing and household services inflation also increased to 4.9% from 4.6%, while food inflation was unchanged at 1.3%. Core CPI inflation held at 2.6%, with goods inflation rising to 2.7% from 2.2% and services inflation unchanged at 3.4%.

UK Producer Inflation Hits 3-Month High — Factory gate prices for UK-manufactured goods increased 3.7% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.3% rise in July, which was the softest increase in four months, and surpassing expectations of 3.3% to mark the fastest increase in three months. 【Preview: Wosh press briefing — can he avoid repeating July’s communication misstep?】Markets will watch whether Wosh clarifies the vague, evasive answers that roiled markets in July; Natixis analysts expect him to frame any rate increase as necessary to return inflation to target. Inflation narrative will be pivotal: if Wosh treats energy and tariff-driven inflation as one-off supply shocks, markets may ease hawkish pricing; if he highlights second-round effects or inflation expectations, tightening risk will rise. His handling of political sensitivity matters — expect scrutiny of any response to Trump’s calls for rate cuts, emphasis on FOMC independence, and whether he stresses data-dependence or avoids naming the president. Watch for changes to his communication posture after a hawkish turn at Jackson Hole and a history of limited forward-rate commentary; renewed insistence on zero tolerance for inflation would lift rate repricing. With the 10y UST yield already above 5% (Sept. 14), traders will also look for comments on further sell-offs and on fiscal- or inflation-risk premia in bond markets.

【Analysis: Limited dollar upside; oil price may be key variable for Fed policy】Market analyst Krishna Kumar says markets have largely priced in a 25 bps Fed hike and dollar upside is limited unless the Fed signals a more hawkish stance. The probability of a September hike has risen to 92%; markets even price three hikes before next March, but much of the recent rise in tightening odds stems from the oil-price spike. The Fed is likely to deliver the scheduled hike to defend anti-inflation credibility, but may avoid explicit forward guidance and is unlikely to validate markets' aggressive pricing of further hikes. Recent remarks from Waller and Williams likewise suggest that, amid high oil-price uncertainty, the Fed may be reluctant to commit to additional tightening. Meanwhile, hawkish shifts at other major central banks could cap dollar gains.

NZX 50 Ends 1% Higher Ahead of GDP Data — The NZX 50 advanced 139 points, or 1.0%, to close at 13,623 on Wednesday, halting losses from the previous four sessions and moving away from its lowest level since June 24, touched a day earlier, ahead of the release of Q2 GDP data. New Zealand's economic data will be released on Thursday, with the market expecting GDP to accelerate 2.9% yoy, while easing to 0.1% qoq. Fresh data also fueled sentiment, as New Zealand consumer confidence moved away from a three-year low, though it remained in pessimistic territory. However, expectations of a Fed interest rate hike, amid higher oil prices, pressured sentiment. The index was also capped by a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight due to a surge in US Treasury yields. Financials, healthcare, and utilities mainly drove the index, with top movers including Ventia Services (4.3%), Infratil (3.6%), Brisco (3.5%), Contact Energy (2.5%), Delegat Group (2.2%), Fisher & Paykel (1.9%), Meridian Energy (1.7%), and Mainfreight (1.2%).

### 2026-09-16 13:40:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kyrgyzstan #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Kyrgyzstan Industrial Output Contracts in August — Industrial production in Kyrgyzstan fell 9.6% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 6.3% increase in the previous month and marking the first decline after ten consecutive months of annual growth. Production dropped in manufacturing (-11.2% vs 0.4% in July), weighed down by declines in food products, textiles, computers, electronic and optical equipment, and other manufacturing, repair, and installation of machinery and equipment. Output also declined in mining and quarrying (-3.6% vs 38.5%), as well as electricity, gas, steam, and air conditioning supply (-9.8% vs 4.7%). Meanwhile, growth eased in water supply, waste treatment, and recovery of secondary raw materials (19.6% vs 20.3%). Over January-August, industrial output expanded 8.8%, slowing from 12.9% in the same period last year.

【李家超：香港特區首個五年規劃聚焦四大中心、人才集聚高地建設】香港特區行政長官李家超16日在特區立法會公佈香港特區第一個五年規劃。他表示，香港將強化四大中心、人才集聚高地建設，鞏固提升國際競爭優勢。李家超表示，全力推進“四中心、一高地”的建設，有助推動香港經濟持續發展，釋放促進民生改善的發展紅利，不斷提升市民福祉。同時，深化國際法律及解決爭議中心、區域智慧財產權貿易中心，以及中外文化藝術交流中心等將為“四中心、一高地”建設提供有力支援，有利促進科研創新和產業發展，並充分發揮香港在“一國兩制”下“內聯外通”的獨特優勢和國際化城市地位，進一步提升國際競爭優勢。李家超說，金融是香港的根本和最大的優勢。香港國際金融中心必須彰顯國際定位，以開放的金融市場吸引全球資金、人才和財金企業匯聚香港。秉持“以穩促進，以進固穩”的方針，壯根基、穩結構、強功能、控風險，充分發揮金融賦能實體經濟的核心作用，建設安全高效的金融基建，系統性構建多元化資本市場。（新華社）

### 2026-09-16 13:08:26  #其他

【處罰噪聲汙染：廣州擬立法地鐵外放最高罰1000元 勸阻無效的可向公安機關報告】據廣州日報，為加強噪聲汙染的防治及其監督管理，《廣州市噪聲汙染防治規定》法規案擬於10月進行第三次審議並表決。9月16日，廣州市人大常委會在官微公佈《廣州市噪聲汙染防治規定（草案修改稿·徵求意見稿）》（以下簡稱《徵求意見稿》），向社會各界公開徵求意見和建議，截止日期為10月15日。 《徵求意見稿》分別對工業噪聲、夜間連續施工噪聲、機動車炸街擾民、商業經營噪聲、公共場所活動噪聲、室內裝修噪聲等多種噪聲源作出管控要求，並明確法律責任。《徵求意見稿》規定，在公共交通運輸工具內使用電子裝置外放聲音，經公共交通運輸工具司乘人員勸阻仍不改正的，由公安機關說服教育，給予警告，可以處二百元以上一千元以下的罰款。 搭乘地鐵、公交時，有的乘客喜歡將手機外放聲音，不僅影響其他乘客上下站聽取報站聲，而且影響他人安寧。對此，《徵求意見稿》作出管控規定，在公交、地鐵等公共交通運輸工具內使用手機、平板電腦等電子裝置時不得外放聲音。使用電子裝置外放聲音的，公共交通運輸工具司乘人員應當勸阻，勸阻無效的，可以向公安機關報告。違反該規定，經公共交通運輸工具司乘人員勸阻仍不改正的，由公安機關說服教育，給予警告，可以處二百元以上一千元以下的罰款。

【香港將大力招攬AI相關人才】香港特區行政長官李家超今日（16日）公佈《香港特別行政區經濟和社會發展第一個五年規劃（2026—2030年）》，隨即發表《行政長官2026年施政報告》（《施政報告》）。李家超在《施政報告》中宣佈，為更聚焦吸引香港發展所需人才，促進人才交流和培訓，特區政府將推行以下措施： 1、研究在“人才清單”加入更多與AI應用相關的人才類別，豐富現時涵蓋60項本地人才短缺的專業（包括金融、醫療和創科專才等）清單。 2、放寬初創科企人才在“高階人才通行證計劃”（高才通）下的續簽要求。外來人才如在港開辦初創業務，可能因企業創始階段營運未穩而收入較低，申請續簽時面對較大的不確定性。獲指定公營機構支援的初創科企經營者將可在申請高才通續簽時獲豁擴音供公司收入證明等要求，便利他們在港發展。 3、擴大“特邀人士入境便利計劃”，由現時東盟成員國擴充套件至中亞和中東國家及地區，並容許所有政府政策局和部門透過計劃發出邀請，促進經貿往來和交流。 4、新增簽證類別，容許非本地人士來港參與政府政策局和部門認可的短期培訓課程，助力香港發展為區域培訓中心。

Aramco has offered long-term Asian buyers Arab Light, Arab Medium and Arab Heavy loaded off Sohar — outside the Strait of Hormuz — signaling more barrels are being moved out of the Gulf for transshipment. Sources say Aramco has made similar Arab Medium/Heavy supply proposals to Asian buyers at least twice in recent weeks. Satellite tracker Energy Aspects shows daily loadings at Gulf terminals Ras Tanura and Juaymah doubled over the past week to roughly 4.0 mln barrels (about two VLCCs). Vessel tracker Kpler recorded four VLCCs loading at Ras Tanura on Wednesday, totaling about 8.0 mln barrels.

Rupiah Slides for 5th Session Ahead of Fed Decision — The Indonesian rupiah slipped past IDR 17,700 per U.S. dollar on Wednesday, extending its losing streak to five sessions as the dollar index hit a one-month high ahead of the Fed’s policy decision. Markets widely expect the U.S. central bank to deliver its first rate hike in around three years, keeping the greenback supported and weighing on emerging-market currencies. Locally, headwinds persisted, with factory activity still weak in August, fragile household sentiment, and retail sales growth slowing. External risks also intensified as crude oil rose above US$100 per barrel amid mounting Middle East tensions, while elevated global yields and cautious foreign investor mood added pressure on Indonesian assets. These developments could complicate inflation and fiscal management for new Finance Minister Suahasil Nazara. Bank Indonesia will hold a policy meeting next week after leaving its key rate unchanged for a second month in August, following cumulative tightening of 100bp since May.

【Vietnam warns power shortfall from next year may threaten double-digit growth target】Vietnam's Ministry of Industry and Trade told the prime minister that delays in generation and grid projects could produce power shortages from next year and jeopardize the government's double-digit economic growth target. The ministry said only about 5% of power projects under the national growth plan are operational; most remain under development or awaiting approval, hampered by investor selection, land procedures and grid readiness. It said supply should meet demand this year but rising consumption and project delays could leave a roughly 4,200 MW gap and about 2.9 bln kWh shortfall next year; the deficit could widen to about 13,900 MW and 61.2 bln kWh by 2030.

Silver Gains for Second Session — Silver climbed above $64 an ounce on Wednesday, rising for the second straight session as gains in oil prices and bond yields lost momentum ahead of the latest US Federal Reserve policy decision. Oil prices pulled back from multi-month highs after a surprise increase in US crude inventories, although ongoing supply disruptions in the Middle East continued to support prices. Meanwhile, global bond yields steadied after their recent rise as investors turned their attention to several major central bank decisions this week. The Fed is widely expected to raise interest rates by 25 basis points, marking its first hike in around three years as policymakers work to curb inflationary pressures. Markets will also focus on signals regarding another potential increase later this year, with expectations building for a move in October or December. The Bank of Japan is likewise expected to raise borrowing costs this week, while the Bank of England is anticipated to leave rates unchanged.

### 2026-09-16 11:48:15  #其他

【中國天眼FAST發現極短週期、最輕雙中子星系統】近日，依託中國天眼FAST超高探測靈敏度，國家天文臺韓金林研究員領銜的FAST銀道面脈衝星巡天團隊取得重要發現，捕獲一顆處於緊緻軌道的雙中子星系統PSRJ1856-0039。該系統是軌道週期極短、目前已知總質量最輕的雙中子星系統，多項觀測結果精準驗證了廣義相對論，為探索極端天體物理、引力本質與宇宙元素起源提供了關鍵的新目標，相關研究成果2026年9月15日發表於《物理評論快報》（PRL），並被期刊編輯遴選為重點推介成果。（新華社）

Palladium Rebounds From Three-Week Low — Palladium futures rose to around $1,320 per ounce, rebounding from three week lows as a softer US dollar and short covering supported prices ahead of the Federal Reserve's policy decision. 【提前償債省份擴圍#，天津擬提前還債4億元】繼北京、陝西之後，天津也開始提前償債。 記者梳理公開資訊發現，截至目前有北京、陝西、天津三地公開發行了提前償債的相關公告。 近日，天津市財政局釋出《關於提前償還部分天津市政府債券的通知》（下稱《通知》），計劃提前償還2020年天津市政府專項債券（四十二期）面值4.09億元，這一提前償債對應的具體專案是天津茱莉亞學院周邊配套工程建設專案。而償債資金來源為專案周邊土地出讓淨收益。 早在2020年5月，天津市政府發行了上述（四十二期）專項債券，發行規模為13.89億元，發行期限為30年，票面利率為3.77%。其中8.8億元專項債券資金投向了天津茱莉亞學院周邊配套工程建設專案，該專案具體包括建設學院教學樓及配建停車庫、學院濱河公園工程及學院周邊環境工程。（一財）

【周鴻禕說不會再投資新能源汽車】據報道，360集團創始人周鴻禕接受媒體採訪時表示，不會再投資新能源汽車，直言“已經吃過一次虧，不會再入局”。他同時表示，如今人工智慧、具身智慧的機會可能更大，並建議所有創業者都要掌握Agent。 2021年，360宣佈計劃合計出資29億元投資哪吒汽車，全部落地後持股16.59%，成為管理團隊之外的第一大股東，先期投入19億元。周鴻禕當時表態稱，360和哪吒不只是資本合作，要深度繫結。 2022年6月，360釋出公告，以0元轉讓對應未出資10億元的3.53%股權，放棄剩餘增資義務，轉讓完成後保留11.4266%股權，不再繼續追加投資。 哪吒汽車此後經營急轉直下。2024年銷量跌至6.45萬輛，2025年國內單月零售量驟降至11輛，賬面現金僅餘3.2億元，最終申請破產重整。2026年3月，哪吒汽車創始人方運舟、前CEO張勇被列為失信被執行人。

【自然資源部組織開展我國黃巖島海域多學科綜合地質調查】為全面掌握我國管轄海域基礎地質要素與生態環境狀況，自然資源部中國地質調查局“海洋地質十號”船於8月11日至9月8日在我國黃巖島海域開展了多學科綜合地質調查。此次調查融合多學科調查手段，利用單道地震、重磁、走航海流、溫鹽深、地質取樣等方法採集了海洋水文、沉積地層、地球物理場等基礎資料，以及海水、海洋生物、海洋沉積物、海底岩石樣品，為進一步摸清該海域地質地球物理特徵及生態環境現狀，開展海底沉積物型別、地層結構、潛在地質災害、地質構造演化、海洋環境質量評價等研究積累了可靠的第一手資料，也為我國在黃巖島海域開展綜合治理提供了科學依據。

### 2026-09-16 11:23:35  #國際

【印尼下調低品位鎳礦石基準價 1.2%礦石成本近減半】印度尼西亞大幅下調低品位鎳礦石基準價格，為因美伊戰爭承受成本壓力的當地加工商提供緩解。根據週五生效的部長級法規，計算礦物基準價格（HPM）的公式已修改，以降低低品位礦石的最低應付價格。據計算，在新水平下，1.2%鎳礦石的成本幾乎減半。這一變化將惠及高壓酸浸（HPAL）工廠，這些工廠將低品位鎳礦石加工成電動汽車電池行業使用的化學品。由於中東戰爭導致關鍵試劑硫磺價格飆升，HPAL工廠已不堪重負，迫使一些工廠考慮聯合減產。今明兩年將有大量新HPAL產能投產，可能對週三跌至年內最低的鎳價構成更大壓力。它們生產的產品稱為混合氫氧化物沉澱，也可轉化為可儲存在倫敦金屬交易所倉庫的純鎳金屬。

Brent Pulls Back as US Inventories Rise — Brent crude fell below $108 per barrel on Wednesday, retreating from four-month highs after industry data showed a surprise increase in US crude inventories despite widening supply disruptions in the Middle East. API data showed US crude stockpiles surged by 7.14 million barrels last week following a 300,000-barrel decline in the previous week, defying expectations for another drawdown. Meanwhile, oil loadings at Saudi Arabia’s Yanbu port remained suspended following the shutdown of the crucial East-West pipeline. There is still no clear timeline for when operations at the key pipeline, which provides an alternative route around the Strait of Hormuz, will resume, as Iran-backed Houthi militants renewed attacks on Saudi Arabia this week. In Libya, the national oil company also suspended operations at two oilfields and a pumping station amid ongoing protests. Despite the disruptions, Libya’s overall oil output has remained largely stable at roughly 1.4 million barrels per day.

【出境游龍頭遭合同詐騙，涉案金額接近去年全年利潤】凱撒旅業公告披露，其全資孫公司凱撒同盛文化產業發展（北京）有限公司（凱撒文產）在簽署演藝服務合同後，按約支付2695萬元款項，但後續無法與合同相對方取得聯絡，懷疑遭遇合同詐騙，已向佛山市公安局南海分局報案並獲立案。目前案件處於偵查階段，結果尚不確定，可能對公司本期及期後利潤造成不利影響。公司已啟動內部專項核查，將依規追責，並加強風控體系，全力配合警方調查以挽回損失。記者以投資者身份致電凱撒旅業，接線工作人員表示，遭遇被騙一事，公司正在積極追討相關款項，但目前無法判斷款項能否被追回。該工作人員還稱，合同詐騙案在行業內較為常見，公司此前也有安排第三方對合作專案方進行背調，後續是否會追責第三方，要看案件進展。近期公司股價下跌，或與當前旅遊板塊整體不景氣相關。（21世紀經濟報道）

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Holds at 5% — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note traded around 5% on Wednesday, remaining near its highest level since July 2007 as investors awaited the latest Federal Reserve policy decision, with the central bank widely expected to raise interest rates as policymakers seek to contain inflationary pressures. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 92% probability of a 25-basis-point increase, marking the first rate hike in around three years. Traders will also look for signals on another potential rate hike later this year, with expectations rising for a move in October or December. Treasury yields were further supported by elevated oil prices, which increased inflation risks, alongside mounting fiscal concerns in the US. Meanwhile, Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent told Congress on Tuesday that the US fiscal outlook had improved in 2026, adding that discussions on fiscal consolidation would follow.

Hong Kong Stocks Steady Ahead of Fed Decision — The Hang Seng Index was little changed, to around 24,601 on Wednesday, as investors remained cautious ahead of the Federal Reserve’s policy decision later in the day. Sentiment was pressured by elevated US Treasury yields, with the 10-year yield having briefly breached 5% in the previous session, while a stronger US dollar also weighed on Asian risk assets. Oil prices remained elevated following a sharp rise in the previous session amid concerns over disruptions to Saudi crude shipments. Against this backdrop, gains in some technology stocks were insufficient to offset broader weakness, leaving the index modestly lower. For individual stocks, Tencent Holdings was among the technology names in focus after ByteDance launched a major update to its Feishu workplace platform, embedding AI agents more deeply into the service and intensifying competition in enterprise AI. Other notable laggards were Xiaomi (-1.5%), Kuaishou (-1.7%) and Akeso (-3.9%), while Z.AI (4.8%) and MiniMax (4.9%) rose.

### 2026-09-16 10:01:15  #宏觀

【食糖生產許可審查細則公開徵求意見】為進一步規範食糖生產許可審查工作，適應行業產業升級、工藝革新與產品迭代發展新形勢，築牢食糖質量安全防線，近日，市場監管總局在充分調研和廣泛徵求意見的基礎上，依據《中華人民共和國食品安全法》《食品生產許可管理辦法》等法律法規規章和食品安全國家標準，研究起草了《食糖生產許可審查細則（徵求意見稿）》（以下簡稱《細則》），並正式向社會各界公開徵求意見。意見反饋截止到10月16日。 《細則》立足督促企業落實食品安全主體責任、規範食糖生產許可工作、推動食糖產業高質量發展，結合食糖生產實際，拓寬許可範圍，新增紅糖、液體糖、其他糖等細類，實現對食品安全國家標準所列食糖品種的全覆蓋。明確食糖生產場所、裝置設施和工藝、人員和管理制度、試製產品檢驗等要求，從源頭防範食品安全風險。嚴格分裝企業審查要求，規定食糖分裝企業應建立並執行原料供應商審查制度，對其採購的食糖原料制定驗收制度，並對成品嚴格落實檢驗要求。同時，明確規定食糖中不得新增澱粉糖，生產企業不得以複合糖、復配糖漿或預混粉為原料生產加工食糖；紅糖生產應具備從甘蔗處理開始的完整生產工藝；有硫漂工藝的，生產企業應按照標準規定控制二氧化硫殘留量，並建立監控記錄。 下一步，市場監管總局將加快推進《細則》落地實施，持續強化食糖生產環節准入，引導企業誠信自律、規範經營，推動食糖產業高質量發展。

European Gas Advances Amid Supply Concerns — European natural gas prices rose above €81/MWh on Wednesday after falling in the previous session, returning to levels close to 2022 highs as supply concerns persist ahead of the winter heating season. Military tensions have kept the Strait of Hormuz largely inaccessible to commercial shipping, keeping Qatari LNG export flows severely restricted and limiting supplies to Europe. European gas storage facilities are around 68% full, still below the seasonal average at a time when inventories would typically be nearing maximum capacity. Concerns over supply security have been reinforced by JERA Global CEO Yukio Kani, who warned that Europe’s low inventories leave the region vulnerable to prolonged disruptions and stronger competition for LNG cargoes. At the same time, scheduled maintenance at Norwegian gas facilities is temporarily reducing pipeline supplies to Europe, further tightening near-term supply.

Yen Declines for Third Straight Session — The Japanese yen weakened past 155 per dollar on Wednesday, extending its decline for a third consecutive session as the dollar strengthened ahead of an expected interest rate hike by the US Federal Reserve. The yen also came under pressure from rising oil prices, which are increasing import costs for Japan’s oil-dependent economy. Still, the Japanese currency remains supported by expectations of more aggressive policy tightening by the Bank of Japan, recent joint interventions by Tokyo and Washington, and prospects for greater capital repatriation by domestic investors. Markets are currently pricing in roughly an 80% chance of a BOJ rate hike on Friday, with another increase expected by the end of January. Meanwhile, data showed that Japanese exports rose more than anticipated in August, supported by strong demand for AI-related chips despite ongoing supply disruptions stemming from the conflict in the Middle East.

### 2026-09-16 09:53:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Australia Leading Index Stays Subdued — The Westpac–Melbourne Institute Leading Economic Index was flat month-on-month in August, unchanged from the previous month. Meanwhile, its six-month annualised growth rate improved to -0.09% from -0.17% in July. Momentum remained soft but has strengthened since mid-year, with the index now only marginally below trend heading into late 2026 and early 2027. Q2 GDP growth showed the Australian economy holding up better than expected despite a global energy shock and higher interest rates. Activity appears to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling, supported by resilient households and a strong ramp-up in data-centre investment. Westpac expects the RBA to raise rates again but, on balance, to wait for the next comprehensive quarterly inflation update on October 28. As a result, the September meeting is likely to deliver a “very hawkish hold.”

【CAAC and National Data Administration issue civil aviation data governance implementation plan】China’s Civil Aviation Administration (CAAC) and the National Data Administration released an implementation plan to build a civil aviation data governance system. Targets for 2027 include progressively完善 data governance rules, materially upgraded industry data-governance capability, basic open sharing of public data in key areas—operations, travel, logistics and supervision—pilots for market-based circulation of data elements, and demonstration high-value data applications and quality data products. Targets for 2030 call for a more complete institutional framework, full open sharing and authorized commercial operation of industry public data, significantly improved data supply and utilization efficiency, a largely formed data-element market and full release of data-element multiplier effects.

Dollar Firms Up Ahead of Fed Decision — The dollar index climbed to around 99.7 on Wednesday, nearing its highest level in a month as investors awaited the latest Federal Reserve policy decision, with the central bank widely expected to deliver its first rate hike in around three years. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 92% probability of a 25-basis-point rate increase as policymakers seek to contain inflationary pressures. Meanwhile, some analysts cautioned that the dollar could weaken sharply if the Fed does not signal further rate increases or opts to keep rates unchanged. 【Pan Gongsheng: Slower growth in financial aggregates helps keep macro leverage broadly stable】PBOC Governor Pan Gongsheng wrote in Qiushi that growth in China’s financial aggregates has slowed, which supports keeping macro leverage broadly stable. He said the recent rapid rise in macro leverage reflected countercyclical policy that boosted financial aggregate and debt growth while low inflation weighed on nominal GDP. The financing market has shifted from supply constraints to demand constraints; effective financing demand can be met and social financing conditions remain relatively loose. He urged balancing short- and long-term aims and warned against financial aggregate growth outpacing real-economy demand, which could create idle funds, lift macro leverage further, impede clearance of excess capacity and inefficient firms, and hurt economic efficiency.

【Pan Gongsheng: De-emphasize quantitative targets; treat financial aggregates as observational, reference and forward-looking indicators】In a Sept. 16 Qiushi article, PBOC Governor Pan Gongsheng said China will continue transforming its monetary policy framework to build a scientific, stable system. The central bank will optimize intermediate monetary variables and de-emphasize quantitative targets—especially single-channel loan targets—treating financial aggregates mainly as observational, reference and forward-looking indicators. Pan said policy will shift toward price-based tools: improve market-based interest-rate formation, transmission and short-end rate control; refine policy rates and supporting institutions to strengthen the policy-rate role; and expand deposit and loan pricing options to provide more loan-pricing benchmarks. He called for stronger execution and supervision of interest-rate policy, measures to curb excessive industry competition and idle fund circulation, and institutionalized, credible routine policy communication to guide market expectations.

Former premier Taro Aso remains vice president and Shunichi Suzuki stays as secretary-general; policy research chair Kobayashi also retained. 【Pan Gongsheng: Credit growth alone not an adequate measure of financial support for the real economy】On Sept. 16 Qiushi published a signed article by PBOC Governor Pan Gongsheng saying credit growth alone cannot measure the strength of financial support for the real economy. He said technological innovation is complex and better served by a diversified financing structure: tech firms typically pass seed, start-up, growth and mature stages with materially different risk profiles and funding needs, requiring a range of markets and financial ecosystems. Early-stage firms rely mainly on private equity and venture capital; in later stages they can finance via bank loans, bonds and equity. Financial markets have an advantage in funding frontier, high-risk/high-return innovation and provide a healthy substitution and diversion from bank lending, and Pan said this structural change will become the norm.

### 2026-09-16 09:30:52  #觀點 #央行 #其他

【潘功勝：金融對經濟的服務更多體現在結構最佳化、適配度提升上，而不是宏觀金融總量的持續擴張上】9月16日，《求是》刊發央行行長潘功勝的署名文章，題為《深刻認識中國金融結構變遷 提升金融服務實體經濟適配性》。其中指出，過去中國金融總量增長較快，主要與經濟的貨幣化程序和高速增長帶來的較大規模貨幣需求有關。改革開放以來，中國從過去的實物分配為主改為貨幣分配，越來越多的商品、住房藉助貨幣來標註價值，以貨幣為媒介進行交易，融資需求也快速增加，金融總量增長較快。當前，我國經濟從高速增長階段轉向高質量發展階段，貨幣化程序已基本結束，金融對經濟的服務更多體現在結構最佳化、適配度提升上，而不是宏觀金融總量的持續擴張上。從總量的數學關係看，增速是增量與全部存量的比值關係。分子是當前的增量，分母是全部的存量。目前，我國社會融資規模存量超過460萬億元，廣義貨幣餘額超過350萬億元，貸款餘額超過280萬億元，宏觀金融總量規模已經很大。隨著存量的擴大，金融總量增速有所下降也是自然的，這與我國經濟發展階段的轉變是一致的。

### 2026-09-16 09:30:01  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CNH #Impact mixed #CSI300 #Hang Seng Index

【Pan Gongsheng: Slower, higher-quality loan growth may become a new macro normal】In a Sept. 16 signed article in Qiu Shi, PBOC Governor Pan Gongsheng said slower but higher-quality loan growth may become a new macro normal. Outstanding loans exceed 280 tln yuan, with a still-large share tied to real estate and local government financing platforms; those exposures are shrinking rather than expanding. From 2025 through 1H 2026, real estate loan balances fell by more than 2 tln yuan cumulatively. Other sectors’ lending must first offset that decline before aggregate credit can rise. As the economy and credit mix transition, the real economy’s demand for credit is changing; maintaining past overall credit growth rates is difficult and unnecessary. Revitalizing inefficient stock loans and new lending serve essentially the same role in supporting growth.

【潘功勝：貸款降速提質或將成為宏觀執行的新常態之一】9月16日，《求是》刊發央行行長潘功勝的署名文章，題為《深刻認識中國金融結構變遷 提升金融服務實體經濟適配性》。其中指出，貸款降速提質或將成為宏觀執行的新常態之一。當前超過280萬億元的貸款餘額中，房地產、地方融資平臺的貸款佔比仍然較大，這些領域不僅不再增長，反而還在下降。2025年至2026年上半年，房地產貸款餘額累計下降超2萬億元。其他領域的貸款要先填補上這個下降的部分，才能帶來總量上的增長。隨著經濟結構轉型和信貸結構變化，實體經濟需要的信貸增長也在發生變化。全部信貸要保持過去的增速是很難的，也是沒有必要的。對於支撐經濟增長來說，盤活低效存量貸款和新增貸款的意義本質上是相同的。

Australian Dollar Under Pressure Ahead of Fed Decision — The Australian dollar steadied around $0.71, holding its recent decline near a four-week low as a firmer US dollar and higher Treasury yields weighed on the currency ahead of the Federal Reserve’s policy decision. The greenback strengthened as markets widely expected the Fed to raise interest rates by 25 basis points, its first increase in around three years, while the US 10-year Treasury yield climbed above 5%, its highest level since 2007, as surging oil prices and persistent inflation concerns reinforced expectations for tighter policy. Oil prices held near a four-month peak above $105 a barrel after fresh Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia and the postponement of Gulf-Iran talks. Domestically, the Reserve Bank of Australia’s hawkish stance continues to provide some support, with markets pricing an 85% probability of a rate increase to 4.60% at its September 29 meeting. Investors now await comments from Governor Bullock, which is scheduled to appear before Parliament on Friday.

【敦煌種業股價波動，近1個月登上龍虎榜15次，公司回應】9月15日，敦煌種業股價再度跌停。此前一個交易日，公司也出現跌停。9月以來，公司股價出現較為明顯的波動。資料顯示，近一個月，敦煌種業登上龍虎榜15次。對此，敦煌種業方面回應中國證券報·中證金牛座記者，種業是農業的前端，受厄爾尼諾氣候變化訊息影響，近期農業板塊受到資金關注。大田玉米種子從8月底開始進入收穫期，收穫期會持續到10月份。對於市場關注的種業預收款問題，敦煌種業董秘張玉財稱，種子行業基本都會有預收款，但每家公司預收的情況不一樣，行業一般是7月份開始進行。種子通常10月份陸續完成加工，會優先給交了預付資金的經銷商安排發貨。經銷商拿到種子後，省級經銷商會先鋪貨到縣級經銷商，縣級經銷商再去農戶做宣傳。如果訂得早，價格會有優惠。且如果有預付，經銷商的銷售壓力會增加，也會及時進行銷售。(中證金牛座)

【中方強調不能擅將安理會特例當慣例 】當地時間9月15日下午，北京時間今天（9月16日）凌晨，聯合國安理會就葉門及曼德海峽局勢舉行緊急公開會。法國在未同安理會成員充分溝通、未根據議事規則協商一致作出決定的情況下，調整會議發言順序，將與會的葉門代表安排在安理會成員之前發言。而這已是法國本月第二次進行類似安排。中國常駐聯合國代表團臨時代辦孫磊大使就此指出，中方一貫主張安理會在討論有關問題時注意傾聽當事國的意見，中方也尊重安理會主席在主持會議方面的裁量空間。同時，中方認為，作為特例是一回事，形成慣例又是另一回事。在安理會程式性安排上，安理會主席應當尊重長期以來形成的慣例。針對當前葉門及曼德海峽局勢，孫磊大使表示，中方始終支援維護葉門和沙特的主權、安全和領土完整，堅決反對在武裝衝突中襲擊包括能源在內的民用基礎設施，堅決反對任何襲擊民用船隻的行為。 孫磊大使指出，無論分歧多大都應當在談判桌上找出路，而不是在槍炮聲中決高下。當務之急是各方要保持冷靜剋制，立即停火止戰，避免採取加劇緊張局勢的行動，早日重返外交談判的正確軌道。

### 2026-09-16 09:00:36  #Trading Economics #Markets #other #currency #Importance 2

Bitcoin Falls as US Senate Rejects Crypto Bill — Bitcoin fell to about $75,000 in mid-September, near a more than three-week low as investors reacted to the Senate’s failure to advance a major US cryptocurrency regulation bill. The Clarity Act failed to advance after a 49-50 procedural vote, falling short of the 60 votes required, and would have placed primary oversight of digital assets with the Commodity Futures Trading Commission. The setback leaves uncertainty around the regulatory framework for the crypto industry, with potential implications for business investment, the location of crypto firms and institutional participation in the US, analysts said. Bitcoin has also faced pressure from a less supportive macro backdrop, as higher interest rates and tighter liquidity tend to weigh on demand for speculative assets. The Federal Reserve’s policy decision is due later today, with markets widely expecting the central bank to deliver its first rate hike in around three years.

Japanese Shares Steady Amid Strong Trade Data — The Nikkei 225 Index was little changed above 63,400 on Wednesday, lacking a clear direction as investors digested stronger-than-expected trade data while preparing for a key US Federal Reserve policy decision. Japanese exports increased more than anticipated in August, supported by solid demand for AI-related chips despite continued supply disruptions stemming from the conflict in the Middle East. Meanwhile, the US central bank is widely expected to raise interest rates later in the day, while the Bank of Japan is also likely to tighten policy on Friday. Elsewhere, investors continued to weigh rising safety concerns surrounding AI development, although Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang dismissed the need for additional AI security regulations, arguing that market forces will encourage companies to innovate safely. Technology stocks traded mixed, with notable declines in Kioxia (-2.7%) and SoftBank Group (-3.7%), while Taiyu Yuden (0.6%) and Advantest (0.5%) posted gains.

【國家醫保局出臺方案 啟動首批全國統一醫保醫療服務專案目錄編制】今天（16日），國家醫保局正式印發《國家基本醫療保險醫療服務專案目錄（第一批）制定工作方案》，我國將首次制定全國統一的醫保醫療服務專案目錄，進一步規範醫保支付範圍，提升保障公平性與基金使用效能。方案明確，堅持“保基本”定位，綜合考量各地基金承受能力、臨床實際需求，按照“分類制定、動態完善”推進。首批將覆蓋心血管、呼吸、眼科、婦科、放療、麻醉、護理、康復、口腔種植、輔助生殖等13個類別。目錄落地後，將率先應用於跨省異地就醫結算，再逐步和各省支付範圍銜接。方案劃定准入標準，臨床必需、安全有效、價格適宜、基金風險可控的專案方可納入；美容保健、非功能性整容、健康體檢等非疾病治療專案，以及效果不確定、風險較高的專案不予納入。此舉將推動醫保支付更加規範高效，更好守護群眾看病就醫權益。

【Huatai Securities: trading-driven selling has eased initially; incremental capital remains cautious】Huatai Securities said trading-driven selling has shown initial relief but fresh incremental capital remains cautious and market liquidity repair has yet to coalesce. Recent volume contraction mainly reflects reduced domestic trading exposures, margin-financing activity at a cyclical low and private funds’ lower sensitivity to the tech sector, suggesting earlier positions have been partly digested. Leverage stabilization has not produced sustained net inflows; ETF demand is driven by structural rotation. Overall funding conditions are improving mostly via reduced selling pressure rather than broad new inflows, while demand-side funding stress has risen and share unlocks in early October could add pressure.

【Japan Aug exports remain strong as chip shipments surge】Japan's exports in August rose 19.3% YoY, down from July's 23.2% but still elevated, the Finance Ministry said. Imports jumped 28% YoY, above economists' 26.3% forecast, widening the unadjusted trade deficit to ¥1.1 trillion from a revised ¥638.3 billion a month earlier — the fourth consecutive monthly deficit. Electronics components led export gains, with semiconductors up 52% and chipmaking-equipment shipments surging; auto exports also rose. Petroleum import value climbed about 59% YoY while volumes rose 3.6%, reflecting higher oil prices. USD/JPY traded near 160.

### 2026-09-16 08:12:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Pressured Ahead of Fed Decision — Silver held below $64 an ounce on Wednesday, hovering near its lowest level in almost six weeks as investors cautiously awaited the latest US Federal Reserve policy decision. The central bank is widely expected to raise interest rates by 25 basis points, marking its first increase in around three years as policymakers seek to contain inflationary pressures. Traders will also look for guidance on another potential rate hike later this year, with expectations growing for a move in October or December. Japan Trade Deficit Widens Sharply as Imports Surge — Japan’s trade deficit widened significantly to JPY 1,105.6 billion in August 2026 from JPY 294.1 billion a year earlier, slightly below market expectations for a JPY 1,052.6 billion shortfall. It marked the fourth consecutive month of deficit and the largest since January, as imports grew faster than exports. Imports surged 28.0% year-on-year to JPY 11,153.9 billion, following a 27.9% rise in July and exceeding forecasts of 26.3%, marking the strongest growth since November 2022. The increase reflected solid domestic demand, partly supported by the government’s late-2025 stimulus. Crude oil imports jumped 58.7% as Japan diversified energy purchases away from the Strait of Hormuz amid ongoing tensions. Exports rose 19.3% to JPY 10,048.4 billion, topping expectations for 18.2%. Outbound shipments increased for a 12th straight month, supported by a weaker yen and robust demand for AI-related chips, despite supply-chain risks stemming from the Middle East conflict.

【日本出口增長保持強勁，對美出口貢獻主要增幅】日本8月出口增速略有回落，但依舊維持高位，對美出口拉動整體增長。日本財務省週三公佈資料，出口額同比增長19.3%，低於7月23.2%的增幅；該結果高於經濟學家18.4%的預期中值。進口同比增長28%，高於經濟學家預估的26.3%。未經季調的貿易逆差擴大至1.1萬億日元，7月修正後逆差為6383億日元，貿易逆差已連續四個月出現。週三釋出的報告著重指出市場波動對貿易收支造成的影響。8月油價維持在相對高位，布倫特原油均價約每桶88美元，日元匯率持續在1美元兌160日元附近波動，推高了日本原油進口金額。受霍爾木茲海峽襲擊事件增多、美伊雙方局勢持續緊張影響，上週布倫特原油價格突破每桶100美元。9月至今，日元兌美元升值幅度超3%，成為同期亞洲表現最佳的貨幣。按出口目的地劃分，對美出口增長24.9%，對華出口增長20.6%，對歐出口增長11%。

【中金：2027年光通訊行業高景氣度確定性抬升】中金公司研報稱，第27屆光博會熱度較往年有增無減，超4000家企業參展，整體觀眾數近19萬人，較去年同比增長32%。“鎖訂單”、“鎖物料”成為展會調研關鍵詞，2027年需求確定性進一步提高。結合展會調研，部分高速光晶片企業訂單排期已延伸至2028年及以後，光模組訂單能見度覆蓋2027年及以後；頭部光模組廠商積極鎖定DSP、mSAP PCB、高速光晶片等核心物料。預計後續交付差異將更多取決於關鍵物料保障、良率和客戶認證，其中先進製程DSP可能成為最緊缺環節。預計至2028年，NPO在標準和工程方案逐步收斂、良率爬升後有望加速上量，CPO或逐步進入Scale-up；OCS從少數雲廠商自用走向更多AI叢集，帶動2.4T輕相干應用下沉；400G per lane推動器件升級，薄膜鈮酸鋰有望加速進入客戶驗證及應用視窗。

【讓前沿技術更快走進病房 這三種創新可申請價格預立項】國家醫保局15日印發《關於試行高水平新技術新產品醫療服務價格專案預立項制度的通知》，明確探索建立醫療服務價格專案預立項制度，促進重大創新成果規模化應用。以往，新技術、新器械、新耗材都是在透過衛生健康部門或藥監部門審批後，才啟動價格立項流程。而透過探索預立項制度，價格支援的關鍵節點被前移至研發及臨床研究階段。國家醫保局醫藥價格和招標採購司副司長 董朝暉：預立項制度，聚焦三種情況的創新。臨床價值大，診療手段突破的新技術。滿足臨床重大需求，有效解決關鍵領域診療難題，提升診療效率和精度的新裝置。填補診療空白，以患者獲益為主，改變手術方案的新耗材。通知同時明確，普通迭代升級裝置、常規試劑、可直接按耗材藥品收費的產品，不在預立項範圍內。

【原油價格連漲兩日後企穩 沙特供應風險為關注焦點】原油價格在連續兩日上漲後企穩，沙烏地阿拉伯和利比亞的供應中斷，以及歐洲爭相採買原油，都表明全球原油市場供應日益緊張。WTI原油在每桶105美元上方，此前週二觸及5月以來最高點；布倫特原油收於每桶109美元附近。沙特繞過沖突不斷霍爾木茲海峽的東西輸油管道，自上週遭到襲擊後一直處於停運狀態。而利比亞由於關鍵閥門關閉，Hamada油田和al-Tahara油田的生產已經停止。目前尚不清楚沙特輸油管道何時能夠重啟，儘管美國能源部長克里斯·賴特告訴CNBC，停運時間可“以天計算”。受此次中斷影響，沙特阿美公司對一些歐洲客戶延遲石油交付，這引發了一場尋找替代供應的爭奪戰。10月交割的WTI原油原油價格下跌0.3%，至每桶105.47美元。11月布倫特原油結算價上漲2.9%，至每桶108.75美元。

US Futures Edge Up Ahead of Fed Decision — US stock futures edged higher as investors awaited the latest Federal Reserve policy decision, with markets widely expecting the central bank to deliver its first rate hike in around three years. Losses were broad-based, with credit-sensitive AI hyperscalers particularly affected by rising borrowing costs, including Alphabet (-1.3%), Microsoft (-1.6%), Oracle (-3.1%), and Amazon (-2%). 【Deloitte survey: UK employees spend nearly £1bn buying AI tools for work】Deloitte said on Wednesday one in six UK employees have paid out of pocket for AI tools to assist their work, totaling nearly £1bn a year (about $1.4bn). Two-thirds of employees reported trying multiple AI tools and almost a quarter use them daily at work. The survey found employee adoption is outpacing employer provision. Deloitte partner Paul Lee said generative AI has quickly entered everyday work but is largely used for basic tasks; firms that gain most will be those that do more than supply tools and instead guide employee use with clear objectives and appropriate guardrails.

### 2026-09-16 07:28:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Unemployment Rate #Importance 0

India Jobless Rate Falls to 6-Month Low — India’s unemployment rate fell to 5.0% in August 2026 from 5.1% in July, coming in below the 5.2% market expectation and marking the lowest level since February. The decline was driven by rural areas, where unemployment dropped to 4.1% from 4.5%, the lowest level since December, while urban unemployment edged up to 6.8% from 6.7%. The improvement coincided with stronger labor-market participation, with the labor force participation rate rising to 55.6% from 55.4%, while the worker population ratio increased to 52.8% from 52.5%, its highest level since March. The rise in the employment ratio alongside higher labor-force participation points to stronger employment activity behind the decline in unemployment. Female labor-force participation also increased to 34.8% from 34.4%, led by rural participation, which rose to 39.4% from 38.8%.

Brent Hovers at 4-Month Highs — Brent crude held above $108 per barrel on Wednesday, remaining near its highest levels in four months amid broadening supply disruptions in the Middle East. Saudi Arabia reportedly cancelled some shipments after drone attacks forced the closure of the East-West pipeline, notifying European customers that several September deliveries had been scrapped. There is still no clear timeline for when operations at the key pipeline, which provides an alternative route around the Strait of Hormuz, will resume, as Iran-backed Houthi militants renewed attacks on Saudi Arabia this week. In Libya, the national oil company also suspended operations at two oilfields and a pumping station amid ongoing protests. Beyond the Middle East, Russia struck petrol stations in Kyiv, while Ukraine targeted a Russian oil refinery, despite US President Trump’s announcement that both sides had agreed to halt attacks on each other’s energy infrastructure.

### 2026-09-16 07:03:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Rises Ahead of GDP Data — The NZX 50 climbed 72 points, or 0.5%, to 13,556 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous four sessions and moving away from its lowest level since June 24, touched a day earlier ahead of the release of Q2 GDP data. New Zealand's economic data will be released on Thursday, with the market expecting GDP to accelerate 2.9% yoy, while easing to 0.1% qoq. Fresh data also lifted sentiment, as New Zealand consumer confidence moved away from a three-year low, though it remained in pessimistic territory. However, expectations of a Fed interest rate hike later this week, amid higher oil prices, pressured sentiment. The index was also capped by a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight due to a surge in US Treasury yields. Financials, real estate, and consumer staples mainly boosted the index, with notable gains from Infratil (5.2%), Vector (2.5%), Sanford (1.5%), Delegat Group (0.7%), and Scales Corp. (0.6%).

【攜程2026Q2淨利潤同比下降，受處罰費用影響，錄得淨虧損24億元】2026年第二季度，攜程集團實現淨營業收入人民幣157億元，同比增長6%；經調整EBITDA為人民幣46億元，較去年同期的49億元有所下降。 本季度，因國家市場監督管理總局反壟斷處罰，公司確認約52億元一次性費用，因此錄得淨虧損24億元；若不考慮反壟斷處罰影響，淨利潤為27億元。 分業務來看，住宿預訂營業收入為66億元，同比增長6%；若剔除處罰相關抵減收入因素，同比增長8%。交通票務營業收入為54億元，同比下降1%；旅遊度假業務營業收入為12億元，同比增長8%；商旅管理業務營業收入為7.71億元，同比增長11%。

### 2026-09-16 05:18:14  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Freight to ship U.S. crude to Asia jumps to $44.8m, a record】Freight to move U.S. crude to Asia hit a record ~$44.8m as Middle East supply disruption fears intensified, Baltic Exchange data showed. Chartering a VLCC to carry 2m barrels from the U.S. Gulf of Mexico to Asia cost about $44.8m on Tuesday, up from $39.0m a day earlier and versus roughly $17.8m prior to the outbreak of war involving Iran. Saudi Arabia’s closure of its east‑west pipeline and elevated Strait of Hormuz transit risk have boosted demand for U.S. barrels as a supply substitute; despite sharply higher freight, WTI remains competitive on arrival in Asia, keeping buyers engaged. Kpler shows six VLCCs slated to load in October from the U.S. Gulf bound for Asia.

### 2026-09-16 05:16:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Stocks Surge — US crude oil inventories rose sharply by 7.14 million barrels in the week ending September 11th, following a 300,000-barrel decline in the previous week. Commercial crude inventories excluding the SPR have fallen by just over 41 million barrels over the past 22 weeks, while total US crude inventories are up nearly 10 million barrels year to date. Another 400,000 barrels were released from the SPR during the week, bringing total SPR holdings to 285 million barrels. The generally accepted operational minimum for the SPR is between 250 million and 300 million barrels. Cushing inventories fell by 246,000 barrels, following a 300,000-barrel decline the previous week. US production rose to 13.947 million bpd in the week ending September 4th, from 13.862 million bpd a week earlier. Gasoline inventories increased by 1.46 million barrels, while distillate inventories gained 1.61 million barrels.

【UN Security Council holds emergency meeting on Bab el‑Mandeb tensions】On Sept 15 the UN Security Council held an emergency meeting on rising instability in the Bab el‑Mandeb. A UN Assistant Secretary‑General reported that fighting on Yemen’s west coast has escalated in recent days and that tensions in the Bab el‑Mandeb and the Strait of Hormuz remain high. The UN warned further escalation could seriously affect international peace and security, global energy markets, food security and multiple national economies. The UN urged restraint and constructive participation in UN‑led political efforts to resolve the Yemen conflict. The meeting was requested by Bahrain, Denmark, France, Greece, Latvia and the UK.

Uruguay Economy Shrinks in Q2 2026 — Uruguay’s GDP declined 0.5% year over year in the second quarter of 2026. The agriculture, fisheries and mining sectors contracted 22.2%, reflecting a poor soybean yield in the 2025/2026 harvest season due to drought, as well as a challenging comparison with the previous season, which ended with a historically high yield. Manufacturing grew 1.9%, driven by stronger cellulose pulp production. Trade, accommodation, and food and beverage services increased 2.8%, supported by the performance of the trade sector. Construction grew 4.2%, driven by increased investment in both nonresidential buildings and other construction projects.

He said shipping through the Strait of Hormuz is recovering, Iran's control there is weakening, and current transit has returned to over 50% of normal, easing near-term oil-flow risk. Vance said the U.S. is not conducting offensive operations now but is responding to Iranian attacks on commercial vessels. He outlined a two-phase campaign: first to destroy Iran's nuclear program, degrade its conventional forces and regional strike capability; second to prevent Iran from rebuilding those capabilities and to maintain regional stability. He said the trajectory will depend on whether Trump expands operations after the midterms or Iran seeks a deal.

Brent Rally Intensifies on Middle East Risks — Brent crude rose to around $108.5 a barrel on Tuesday, its highest level in four months, after Saudi Arabia reportedly cancelled some shipments following drone attacks that forced the closure of its key export pipeline. Saudi Arabia reportedly informed European customers that some September deliveries were cancelled, while oil loadings at Yanbu were suspended. Meanwhile, Iran-backed Houthi militants renewed strikes on Saudi Arabia this week. Security in the Strait of Hormuz remains volatile, with at least two tankers attacked since Saturday. Commodity vessel traffic through the strait fell to four on Monday from 10 a day earlier, Kpler data showed. In Libya, the national oil company suspended operations at two oilfields and a pumping station amid protests. Russia also struck petrol stations in Kyiv and Ukraine targeted a Russian oil refinery, despite US President Trump’s announcement that both sides had agreed to halt attacks on each other’s energy infrastructure.

【美國參議院否決了加密貨幣法案】週二，美國參議院否決了一項覆蓋面廣泛的加密貨幣法案，遭到民主黨及少數共和黨議員反對。歷經數月兩黨激烈談判，這項法案最終以慘敗收場。參議院以49票贊成、50票反對的結果否決了所謂的《清晰法案》（Clarity Act），給加密行業帶來沉重打擊。民主黨表示，法案未能充分解決特朗普總統在數字資產領域潛在的利益衝突問題；部分共和黨議員則受到銀行業相關顧慮的影響。所有民主黨議員均投下反對票，法案因此失去在參議院闖過首輪程式性障礙所必需的兩黨支援。約十幾名民主黨參議員耗時數月參與該法案談判，但週二無一人投出贊成票；他們認為法案中的政府道德約束條款與消費者保護條款力度不足。

【CBO: Iran war cost US $38 bln in five months; will lift inflation 0.5 percentage point early next year】The Congressional Budget Office estimates the Trump administration’s hostilities with Iran cost U.S. taxpayers about $38 billion in the first five months, driven by munitions and equipment replacement, increased flight hours, other operations and fuel. CBO says each additional month will add at least $2–3 billion, with higher costs if the conflict escalates. It warns disruption to oil and gas shipments through the Strait of Hormuz will raise U.S. inflation: the Fed’s preferred inflation gauge could be 0.5 percentage point higher in early 2027 versus prior projections, with core PCE about 0.3 percentage point higher. The report adds the war has depleted U.S. ammunition stocks, some of which may take more than five years to replenish, potentially weakening U.S. ability to respond to other major conflicts; a future high-volume missile/ammunition conflict could strain defense production capacity and inventory replenishment rates.

Gasoline Rises to Eight-Week High — US gasoline futures rose above $3.45 a gallon, rising to an eight-week high, amid a tightening outlook for energy supply from the Middle East. Attacks last week by Iran-backed Houthi rebels shut down a crucial crude pipeline in Saudi Arabia that bypasses the Strait of Hormuz, which remains under blockade. Since the start of the US-Iran war, the crack spread has surged from the teens to above $60, reflecting demand for refined products outpacing refiners’ ability to produce them. Refiners had long warned about the impact of depleting crude reserves, and although operating near full capacity, delays to non-essential work have compounded the maintenance backlog ahead of seasonal fall maintenance, posing an ongoing threat to refined-product supplies. While UK authorities fear the pipeline could remain shut for as much as six weeks, US Energy Secretary Wright told reporters that it would reopen "very soon".

【Massie files impeachment resolution against Defense Secretary Hegseth over Iran operations】Republican Representative Thomas Massie on Tuesday filed an impeachment resolution accusing Defense Secretary Hegseth of continuing U.S. military action against Iran without congressional authorization. The measure contains eight articles and also cites U.S. operations in Venezuela and Yemen. As a privileged resolution, Massie can force a House vote within a prescribed period, compelling Republicans to take a position on Hegseth's wartime conduct; passage is unlikely in a GOP-controlled Congress. The House has previously voted on measures to curb executive war powers on Iran and will again consider a War Powers resolution this week.

### 2026-09-16 00:41:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Extends Rally — Brent crude rose past $108 per barrel on Tuesday, the highest in over four months, on mounting curbs to supply from the Middle East and fresh setbacks on Libyan output. Saudi Arabia closed its East-West pipeline following attacks from Iraqi territory. The strikes suspended activity in infrastructure that was allocating 7 million barrels per day of Saudi oil to the Red Sea, an alternative to halted shipments through the Persian Gulf due to the Iranian blockade on tankers. The prolonged halt of tanker flows from the region forced major OPEC members to cut oil production, with Saudi output recently dropping to its lowest since 1990. Meanwhile, Libya suspended activity in several oil fields and warned it could declare force majeure as protests by the Petroleum Facilities Guard shut off the Hamada-Zawiya crude-loading pipeline. Dwindling stockpiles of oil in China drove the major importer to raise orders in August, while the US SPR stands at a near record low.

Israel Inflation Rate Holds Steady — Israel’s annual inflation rate stood at 1.5% in August 2026, unchanged from the previous month and slightly below the expected 1.6%. Inflation remains comfortably within the central bank’s 1%-3% target range, as the reading remains at its lowest level since May 2021. Price growth rebounded for transport (0.6% vs -0.4%), while deflation eased for furnishings (-3.9% vs -4.1%) and clothing and footwear (-5.2% vs -6.3%). Meanwhile, price pressures eased for food, including vegetables (1% vs 1.4%), housing (3.8% vs 3.9%), dwelling maintenance (1.4% vs 1.6%), health (1.5% vs 1.6%), and education, culture and entertainment (0.8% vs 1.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.7%, accelerating from a 0.3% increase in the previous month.

【Bessent backs Trump $5,000-per-adult payout plan, says it can be done without widening deficit】Treasury Secretary Bessent told lawmakers Tuesday he supports President Trump’s proposal to send $5,000 checks to U.S. adults if Republicans retain control of both chambers. He downplayed cost concerns, saying he believes the payments can be arranged without increasing the fiscal deficit, but did not identify offsets. Bessent said the Treasury has been working on the proposal for some time and did not state whether congressional authorization would be required; if it is, he said he would work with House Speaker Johnson to advance it. Markets have growing concern over rising U.S. government debt. The plan, promised by Trump less than a week earlier, is estimated to cost more than $1 trillion.

FTSE 100 Falls on Tuesday — The FTSE 100 fell 0.4% on Tuesday after gaining 0.4% in the previous session, as rising oil prices and weak UK employment data weighed on sentiment. Brent crude traded near $108 a barrel, while investors assessed how long Saudi Arabia’s key oil pipeline could remain closed. Meanwhile, UK payrolls fell by 26,000 in August following a revised 19,000 decline in July, far exceeding expectations. The data highlighted continued labour-market weakness ahead of Thursday’s Bank of England rate decision. Unemployment remained at 4.9% in the three months to July, while regular wage growth held at 3.5%. Banks led declines, with HSBC, Barclays and Lloyds falling 2.2%, 1.9% and 0.6%, respectively. Miners also weakened, led by Glencore’s 2.5% drop.

### 2026-09-16 00:02:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Hits Four-Month Low — The CAC 40 index dropped 0.3% to close at 8,090 on Tuesday, extending losses from the previous session and hitting a four-month low as rising oil prices and global bond yields weighed on equities. Oil prices climbed after attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure, raising inflation concerns and expectations for interest-rate hikes. Higher yields also pressured equities after the US 10-year Treasury yield reached 5%. In France, the 10-year government bond yield climbed to its highest level since September 2008, during the global financial crisis. CPI data showed annual inflation up to 2.4% in August from 2.1% in July, reaching a three-month high. Luxury stocks led the downturn, pressured by weaker Chinese demand and reduced access to the Middle East travel hub. LVMH dropped 2.6%, Hermes fell 2.8%, and Kering shed 1.5%. Financials also posted losses, with BNP Paribas losing 1.5%. Health-care stocks declined as well, with EssilorLuxottica down 2.2% and Sanofi losing 1.6%.

### 2026-09-16 00:01:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Lumber Falls to Three-Week Low — Lumber futures traded near $565 per thousand board feet, falling toward its lowest level in three weeks, amid an ongoing trade war between Canada and the US. Canadian Prime Minister Carney is turning to the Europe Union after walking away from trade talks with US President Trump, which accounted for the largest portion of its trade. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on Plywood following US tariffs on several lumber-related goods. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, as 94% of Canadian forestland is publicly owned compared to 58% privately owned in the US, allowing Canadian lumber to be sold at a lower price. Meanwhile, mortgage rates are closing in on 7% for the first time since the beginning of 2025, further weighing on housing demand from prospective home buyers already wincing at record-high home prices and an uncertain economy.

Peru Economy Grows 3.56% in July — Peru’s economy expanded 3.56% year-on-year in July 2026, accelerating from a 1.75% increase in June and marking the strongest growth since April. Commerce grew 6.79%, driven by a 4.22% increase in wholesale trade, a 28.77% surge in automotive trade, and a 6.02% rise in retail trade. Other services, which include real estate and personal services, expanded 5.08%. Construction grew 9.85%, supported by a 10.17% increase in domestic cement consumption and a 9.51% rise in the physical progress of construction works. Business services (4.24%), financial services (3.13%), public administration and defense (3.46%), transportation (2.86%), utilities (7.30%), accommodation and restaurants (3.48%), mining and hydrocarbons (0.24%), and telecommunications (0.30%) also expanded. In contrast, manufacturing (-1.81%), fishing (-27.44%), and agriculture (-1.23%) contracted.

### 2026-09-15 23:16:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Tops 5% — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note rose for a fifth consecutive session on Tuesday, hitting the closely watched 5% threshold for the first time since July 2007. The yield climbed as high as 5.04% before easing slightly to 5.01%. The renewed rise in oil prices, which pushed crude back above $100 a barrel, continues to cloud the inflation outlook, particularly as tensions in the Middle East remain elevated and risks to oil supplies increase. In addition, the Fed is widely expected to raise interest rates for the first time since 2023 on Wednesday. A decision to leave rates unchanged could put further upward pressure on Treasury yields, as markets may interpret such a move as undermining the Fed’s credibility in its fight against inflation. US headline inflation held at 3.4% in August, while the core inflation rate stood at 2.4%. Investors will also closely scrutinize the Fed’s updated economic projections, particularly its outlook for interest rates.

### 2026-09-15 23:16:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Botswana #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Botswana Inflation Rate Edges Down to 9.3% — Botswana's annual inflation rate ticked down to 9.3% in August 2026 from 9.4% in the prior month and decelerating further from a recent peak of 10.7%. Price growth moderated a bit for transportation (19.7% vs 19.9%); food & non-alcoholic beverages (5.9% vs 6.6%); alcoholic beverages & tobacco (10% vs 10.7%) and miscellaneous goods & services (11.6% vs 11.7%). On the other hand, inflation remained steady for restaurants & hotels (at 5.3%), but accelerated for housing & utilities (4.8% vs 3.9%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices went up by 0.4%, after decreasing by 1.8% in the month before.

【韓正出席第十三屆北京香山論壇歡迎晚宴並致辭】9月15日，國家副主席韓正出席第十三屆北京香山論壇歡迎晚宴並致辭。韓正表示，當前世界百年變局加速演進，國際形勢變亂交織。面對世界之變、時代之變、歷史之變，習近平主席提出構建人類命運共同體理念和四大全球倡議，為解決人類面臨的共同問題指明瞭方向。儘管當今世介面臨複雜挑戰，但和平、發展、合作、共贏的時代潮流不可阻擋，世界多極化、經濟全球化的大勢不可逆轉，各國人民要求實現發展和安全的願望更加迫切，追求國際公平正義的呼聲更加強烈。韓正提出四點建議。一是堅持維護聯合國憲章宗旨和原則，堅持主權平等，反對干涉內政，反對動輒使用武力或以武力相威脅，防止世界退回弱肉強食的叢林法則。二是堅持真正的多邊主義，尊重各國人民自主選擇的發展道路和社會制度，尊重各國合理安全關切，構建更具包容性、公平性、可持續性的全球治理模式。三是堅持透過對話協商化解分歧和爭端，支援一切有利於和平解決危機的努力，以對話增互信、解紛爭、促安全，防止衝突升級和蔓延。四是堅持以人為本、科技向善，面對蓬勃發展的新興技術，要加快完善人工智慧、網路空間、外空等新興領域治理規則，加強開放合作，服務全球和平與發展事業。(新華社)

【摩根大通調查顯示：美債客戶大舉湧入空頭頭寸】摩根大通對美國國債市場投資者的一項調查顯示，在過去一週內，客戶以2019年以來最快的速度湧入看空頭寸。截至9月14日當週，持有空頭頭寸的客戶比例上升了10個百分點至19%，創下自2019年有記錄以來的最大單週增幅。中性頭寸從56%降至48%，為2023年11月以來的最低水平。多頭頭寸從35%降至33%。這一轉變使得參與者的淨多頭頭寸規模降至5月18日以來的最低水平。空頭頭寸的積累緊隨9月11日公佈的8月消費者價格指數（CPI）資料之後——該資料顯示核心價格環比上漲0.3%，高於預期。這一通脹資料增強了美聯儲在9月16日加息的理由。

Oil prices remained well above $100 a barrel after attacks on Saudi Arabia’s energy infrastructure forced the East-West Pipeline offline, heightening concerns over global supply. Geopolitical risks also intensified amid continued disagreements between the US and Iran over the Strait of Hormuz, alongside an increased Houthi presence near the Strait of Bab el-Mandeb. Markets are pricing in a 25-basis-point rate hike by the Fed on Wednesday, with investors closely watching policymakers’ guidance for signs of further tightening. ### 2026-09-15 22:39:05  #其他

【南非總統呼籲非洲國家加強發展綠色氫能】為期兩天的2026年非洲綠色氫能峰會15日在南非立法首都開普敦開幕。南非總統拉馬福薩呼籲非洲國家抓住綠色氫能發展的歷史性機遇，加強務實合作，以實現能源安全、推動脫碳程序並促進可持續發展。 拉馬福薩在由該國電力和能源部長科西恩喬·拉莫豪帕代為宣讀的開幕致辭中說，非洲擁有豐富的可再生能源、水資源和關鍵礦產，完全有條件成為全球綠色氫能產業重要樞紐。發展綠色氫能不僅關係到投資和新興產業佈局，也有助於緩解非洲能源貧困、創造就業，並推動公正能源轉型。非洲應抓住這一千載難逢的機遇，加快行動步伐，以躋身變革性工業發展的最前沿。 拉馬福薩強調，非洲不能僅作為可再生能源、礦產和氫能產品的供應地，還應參與整個價值鏈。他呼籲技術合作夥伴在非洲構建制造、培訓和研究能力，並擴大本地採購、製造、就業和技能轉移。 拉馬福薩宣佈，南非在國家綠色氫能計劃下推出首批6個綠色氫能專案。其中，位於西開普省薩爾達尼亞灣的電制可持續航空燃料專案預計2027年第一季度開工建設，2029年第一季度出口首批電制可持續航空燃料。 非洲綠色氫能峰會的前身是2022年開始舉辦的南非綠色氫能峰會，2025年起峰會範圍擴大到整個非洲。會議期間，政府官員、能源專家、行業領袖、決策者和投資者等齊聚一堂，探討非洲綠色氫能產業發展的未來。(新華社)

Angola Trims Key Policy Rate to 14.75% — The National Bank of Angola slashed its key interest rate by 100 bps to 14.75% at its August 2026 meeting, marking the third consecutive rate cut.to the lowest since 2016. Policymakers cited the sustained disinflationary trend and an improving economic outlook, despite uncertainty stemming from the conflict in the Middle East. The annual inflation rate fell to 8.78% in August 2026 from 9.33% in July, marking the lowest since April 2015 and extending the downward trend observed since mid-2024. Looking ahead, Governor Manuel Tiago Dias said that inflation is still expected to end the year at 8.6%. Meanwhile, economic growth accelerated sharply to 8.74% year-on-year in Q2 2026, marking its strongest expansion since Q2 2022, up from 5.32% in Q1.

Swiss Bond Yield Eases from Multi-Month High — Switzerland’s 10-year government bond yield eased to around 0.58% after reaching its highest level since March 2026, as the OECD raised its economic growth forecast. The OECD stressed the need for tax and pension reforms, as the country’s aging population and growing geopolitical challenges are expected to create long-term spending pressures. Still, the organization raised its economic growth forecast to 2% from 1.1%, following a strong performance in the second quarter that reached 1.5%, its highest level in five years, driven by a weaker Swiss franc that supported exporters. Safe-haven demand also provided support amid rising oil prices and heightened economic uncertainty. On the monetary policy front, a Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all bankers expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% by year-end, as the impact of higher energy prices remains limited. Markets anticipate the first rate hike in June 2027, while most economists expect the first hike in early 2028.

Dominican Inflation Eases in August — The annual inflation rate in the Dominican Republic eased to 5.13% in August 2026 from 5.47% in July. The strongest price pressures were recorded in restaurants and hotels (7.96%), transport (7.36%), education (7.08%), and food and non-alcoholic beverages (5.90%). In contrast, prices fell for clothing and footwear (-1.08%) and communications (-1.76%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.38%, accelerating from a 0.19% increase in the previous month. Year-to-date inflation reached 2.60%. Meanwhile, annual core inflation eased to 4.76% from 4.96% in July.

### 2026-09-15 22:18:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Nigeria #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Nigeria Inflation Rate Cools Down for 3rd Month — The annual inflation rate in Nigeria eased slightly to 15.39% in August 2026 from 15.43% in July, marking the lowest since March and the third straight month of slowdown. The relative strength of the naira provided support. Food inflation, the largest component of the inflation basket, fell to 19.57% from 20.31% in July, marking the first monthly decline in seven months. Price growth also softened for other CPI items, particularly transportation (12% vs 15.5% in July); restaurants & hotels (18.6% vs 21.8%); miscellaneous goods & services (12.7% vs 15.6%) and clothing & footwear (1.9% vs 4%). Conversely, prices picked up for housing & utilities (16.9% vs 12.6%). Meanwhile, the core inflation rate, which strips out the volatile prices of agricultural produce and energy, also moderated for the third month to the lowest since March 2021 at 13.29%, down from July's 14.97%. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.71% in August, the least in seven months, after a 1.57% increase in July.

【DeepSeek工程師也有AI焦慮：不至於失業，但必須轉業】在AI的洪流下，連DeepSeek的頂尖工程師也要擔心自己未來的事業了。 DeepSeek機器學習系統（MLSys）工程師劉勝與發表長文《我不得不把才華埋葬在昨天》，他在文章中提到，面對AI能力的快速進步，自己未來不至於會“失業”，但可能必須要“轉業”。 這一焦慮來自一名站在AI浪潮前沿的工程師。從個人簡歷來看，劉勝與自2025年4月起在DeepSeek從事機器學習系統及運算元設計與最佳化工作。在此之前，他是北京大學2021級圖靈班學生，曾任北京大學超算隊隊長，並帶隊在世界級大學生超級計算機競賽中取得前兩名。 前幾天DeepSeek釋出 v4.1新模型，將能力又向前推進了一個檔次。劉勝與提到，DeepSeek v4.1的主Attention運算元是自己寫的，他為DeepSeek v4.1的成功而驕傲。但他很清楚的是，再過上半年或者一年，AI 寫的運算元大機率就會和自己寫得同樣優秀，甚至將自己超越。（第一財經）

【META計劃明年上半年部署新款自研ARKE晶片 展示自研晶片降本優勢】據外媒報道，Meta Platforms(META.O)計劃於明年上半年開始在資料中心部署其新款自研ARKE晶片，該公司表示，此舉將在執行人工智慧模型時節省成本和能源。公司早於2023年首次宣佈了自主研發AI晶片的計劃，目前正在測試該系列的第三代產品，代號為MTIA 450，或稱Arke。下一代產品——代號500，或稱Astrid——將在約一個月內完成設計工作，並於2027年底投入資料中心使用。Meta預計未來會更廣泛地採用這一產品。Meta工程副總裁Jiun Song在接受採訪時表示：“每一代晶片在技術上都承擔著更高的風險，但效能也得到了顯著提升。”報道稱，Meta超級智慧實驗室透過提供對未來AI模型及其執行要求的見解（即推理階段）來協助對晶片進行微調。Song表示，最終結果是，在執行AI模型時，這些晶片的效能將比“英偉達目前推出的任何產品”都更高效，“僅僅是因為我們自己完成了大量工程設計工作。”

【20年期美國國債拍賣收益率或將創紀錄】紐約時間下午 1 點即將進行的 20 年期美國國債再發行拍賣，有望創下該品種自 2020 年重啟發行以來的最高拍賣收益率。長端債券市場出現小幅熊陡拋售，助推收益率上行。本次 130 億美元規模的再發行，發行前交易收益率約 5.41%，將超過 2023 年 10 月 5.245% 的紀錄，成為該品種歷史新高。上週附息國債拍賣在高收益率水平下獲得強勁需求，這對本次 20 年期國債拍賣而言是潛在利好。20 年期國債收益率週二最高觸及 5.44%，為該品種重啟後的歷史高點。上週 10 年期與 30 年期美國國債拍賣，投標收益率分別較發行收益率低 1.4 個基點和 2.7 個基點。摩根大通策略師在週一晚間研報中指出，宏觀環境缺乏吸引力、估值偏高且技術面支撐不足，本次拍賣需要更大的收益率讓利才能順利消化。美國商品期貨交易委員會最新持倉資料顯示，投機者持有 20 萬份傳統國債期貨淨空頭頭寸，或帶來空頭回補需求。

【OpenAI支援國會兩黨AI安全法案，首次贊成強制引入第三方安全評估】OpenAI表示支援美國眾議院一項兩黨人工智慧安全立法中的關鍵條款，該條款要求領先AI公司允許獨立第三方評估機構進入企業，對先進模型的開發和安全措施進行審查。這是OpenAI首次公開支援由聯邦法律強制要求頂級AI實驗室接受第三方安全評估。OpenAI全球事務主管Chris Lehane稱，公司已向法案發起人明確表示，“我們可以支援”獨立驗證機構機制。該提案屬於FRONTIER Act，由共和黨眾議員Jay Obernolte和民主黨眾議員Lori Trahan共同推動，旨在建立美國首個聯邦AI安全框架。OpenAI近期已明顯加強對強制監管的支援，公司此前提出，美國需要建立“強制性的、基於能力的國家AI安全監管”，並將獨立驗證列為核心機制之一。 不過，FRONTIER Act目前仍處於立法推進階段，相關強制要求是否最終成為聯邦法律尚未確定。

US Natgas Prices Rise for 2nd Session — US natural gas prices rose above $2.94 per million British thermal units, reaching their highest level since September 4, driven by lower production and forecasts for continued warm weather. Average output across the Lower 48 states was expected to fall to a two-month low of 108.4 bcfd on Tuesday. Hotter-than-normal conditions have also increased gas consumption for power generation, with analysts estimating that storage was 3.6% above the five-year average in the week ending September 11, down from 4.8% the previous week. Despite record production and mild spring weather keeping inventories above the five-year average since March, the surplus has continued to narrow. Meteorologists expect temperatures to remain mostly above normal through September 30, likely sustaining elevated demand for air conditioning and gas-fired power generation. Meanwhile, average flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities rose to 18.3 bcfd so far in September, up from 17.2 bcfd in August.

US Stocks Hold Decline — US stocks were lower on Tuesday, holding the pullback from the previous session on further pressure from higher bond yields. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.5%, while the Dow lost 1%. Long-maturity yields rose further as the escalation to strikes in the Middle East worsened the outlook on energy supply from the region, magnifying inflation risks and a hawkish response from the Federal Reserve. Markets expect the Fed to hike rates by 25bps tomorrow as fuel prices drove underlying inflation to gain traction this year. Most sectors of stock exchanges were lower, with credit sensitive AI hyperscalers leading the losses as their increasing debt positions clash against higher borrowing costs. Alphabet, Microsoft, and Meta fell 1%. Meanwhile, chip producers rebounded from yesterday's slump as markets assessed how risks to AI safety could slow infrastructure spending, with Nvidia, Lam Research, and Intel gaining 1%.

Wheat Falls to 3-Week Low — Wheat fell towards $7.12 per bushel, the lowest level in three weeks, as renewed hopes for a Russia-Ukraine ceasefire raised expectations of reduced disruption to Black Sea grain shipments. US President Donald Trump announced a truce on strikes on Russia and Ukraine's energy facilities. Also, Turkey is seeking to broker a broader agreement between Kyiv and Moscow that could stop attacks on ships in the Black Sea. Ukraine has indicated it would support such a deal if Russia agrees, while the Kremlin has described the proposal positively. The prospect of improved trade flows has added pressure to wheat prices, which remain below the 3½-year high of $7.67 reached on August 28. Meanwhile, the latest USDA forecasts pointed to slightly above-average global wheat inventories. The agency also lowered its 2026/27 export forecasts for Russia by 3 million metric tons to 43 million tons and for Ukraine by 1 million tons to 12.5 million tons

Baltic Dry Index Down for 4th Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, retreated for a fourth session on Tuesday, dropping about 2.5% to a new low since September 2 at 3,360 points. The downward pressure came from larger vessel segments. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, declined 3.8% to 5,687 points, on higher shipments of iron ore; and the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell 1.2% to 2,364 points. Conversely, the supramax index rose by 0.6% to 1,736 points.

Brent Crude Climbs for 2nd Day — Brent crude rose to around $107.50 a barrel on Tuesday, near a four-month high, following a 1% gain in the previous session, as supply disruptions in the Middle East outweighed signs of progress towards a Russia-Ukraine energy truce. Market concerns remain focused on Saudi Arabia, where the East-West pipeline has been shut, removing an important alternative route for exporting crude without relying on the Strait of Hormuz. Riyadh is reportedly seeking to increase shipments through the strategic waterway while the pipeline remains offline, although repairs could take several weeks. US Energy Secretary Chris Wright has indicated that he expects the pipeline to resume operations soon. Meanwhile, diplomatic efforts are gaining momentum elsewhere. US President Donald Trump announced a truce on strikes on Russia and Ukraine's energy facilities, while Turkey is seeking to broker an agreement between Kyiv and Moscow to end attacks on Black Sea shipping.

Riyadh is reportedly seeking to increase shipments through the strategic waterway while the pipeline remains offline, although repairs could take several weeks. US Energy Secretary Chris Wright has indicated that he expects the pipeline to resume operations soon. Meanwhile, diplomatic efforts are gaining momentum elsewhere. US President Donald Trump announced a truce on strikes on Russia and Ukraine's energy facilities, while Turkey is seeking to broker an agreement between Kyiv and Moscow to end attacks on Black Sea shipping.
